Support Matrix
#
This support matrix provides filterable access to TensorRT compatibility information across all releases from 10.0.0 EA through 11.3.0. Use the three explorers below to find information about system requirements, hardware capabilities, and feature support.
Software and System Requirements
#
Use this when you need to know what to install or what versions are required

Engine Portability
Platform:
Serialized engines are not portable across platforms (Linux, Windows, etc.).
Version Compatibility:
Engines built with the version-compatible flag can run with newer TensorRT versions within the same major version.
Hardware Compatibility:
Engines built with hardware compatibility mode can run on multiple GPU architectures, depending on the hardware compatibility level used. Without this mode, engines are not portable across different GPU architectures.
Find out what you need to install and run TensorRT.
First select your TensorRT version to view software requirements by platform group (CUDA, NCCL, drivers, and more). Optionally select a platform in Step 2 to view compiler versions, Python requirements, compute capabilities, driver requirements, and framework compatibility.

#
Use this when you need to know hardware capabilities and precision support

#
Use this when you need to know which TensorRT features are available in which versions
Find out which TensorRT features and components are supported in which versions.
Filter by TensorRT version and component to see feature availability across releases. Use this to determine if a specific feature is available in your target TensorRT version.

JavaScript is required to use the interactive Support Matrix.
         Please enable JavaScript in your browser to use the interactive Support Matrix.

   function getPlatformListForVersion(versionData) {
      if (!versionData) return [];
      var platforms = versionData.compilerPython.map(function(item) { return item.platform; });
      if (versionData.hideGenericJetPack) {
         platforms = platforms.filter(function(platform) {
            return platform !== 'NVIDIA JetPack AArch64';
         });
      }
      if (versionData.embeddedPlatforms) {
         platforms = platforms.concat(Object.keys(versionData.embeddedPlatforms));
      }
      return sortPlatforms(platforms);
   }

   function renderEmbeddedPlatformView(platformFilter, versionData) {
      var info = versionData.embeddedPlatforms[platformFilter];
      if (!info) return '';
      var html = '<div class="nv-data-table-container">';
      html += '<h3 class="nv-embedded-heading">' + platformFilter + '</h3>';
      html += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--wide"><tbody>';
      html += '<tr><td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">TensorRT version</td><td class="nv-data-table__td">' + info.tensorRT + '</td></tr>';
      html += '<tr><td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">CUDA requirement</td><td class="nv-data-table__td">' + info.cuda + '</td></tr>';
      html += '<tr><td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">DLA</td><td class="nv-data-table__td">' + info.dla + '</td></tr>';
      html += '<tr><td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Notes</td><td class="nv-data-table__td">' + info.notes + '</td></tr>';
      html += '<tr><td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Migration guidance</td><td class="nv-data-table__td">';
      if (!info.migrationHref || info.migrationLabel === 'N/A') {
         html += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
      } else {
         html += '<a href="' + info.migrationHref + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + info.migrationLabel + '</a>';
      }
      html += '</td></tr>';
      html += '</tbody></table>';
      if (info.relatedPlatform) {
         html += '<p class="nv-callout-text">For CUDA, compiler, and Python requirements, select <strong>' + info.relatedPlatform + '</strong> in Step 2.</p>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
      return html;
   }

   function renderJetPack11xCallout(versionFilter) {
      var is113 = (versionFilter === '11.3.0');
      var is112 = (versionFilter === '11.2.1');
      var dlaNote = is113
         ? 'DLA is not supported in TensorRT 11.3.0 or in TensorRT 11.3.1 for DriveOS. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA. '
         : (is112
            ? 'DLA is not supported in TensorRT 11.0, 11.1, or 11.2. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA. '
            : 'DLA is not supported in TensorRT 11.0 or 11.1. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA. ');
      var jetPackNote = is113
         ? 'NVIDIA JetPack is not supported in TensorRT 11.3.0. Jetson deployments must remain on a supported TensorRT 10.x release. '
         : (is112
            ? 'NVIDIA JetPack is not supported in TensorRT 11.2.1. Jetson deployments must remain on a supported TensorRT 10.x release. '
            : 'Jetson Orin/Thor (JetPack 7.x) supports TensorRT 11.x; Jetson Orin (JetPack 6.x) supports TensorRT 10.x. ');
      var jetLinkLabel = (is113 || is112)
         ? ('TensorRT ' + versionFilter + ' support status on Jetson/JetPack')
         : 'Migrating TensorRT from 10.x to 11.x on Jetson/JetPack';
      return '<div class="nv-engine-callout">'
         + '<p class="nv-callout-text" style="margin:0;">'
         + '<strong>Jetson platform eligibility:</strong> ' + jetPackNote + dlaNote
         + 'Select an entry under <strong>Embedded platforms</strong> in Step 2 for eligibility details, or refer to '
         + '<a href="../api/migration/tensorrt-10x-to-11x-jetson.html" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + jetLinkLabel + '</a>.'
         + '</p>'
         + '<p class="nv-callout-text nv-callout-text--spaced">'
         + '<strong>DriveOS platform eligibility:</strong> DRIVE Thor (DriveOS 7.2.6+) supports TensorRT 11.x'
         + (is113
            ? '. DLA is not supported in TensorRT 11.3.0 or in TensorRT 11.3.1 for DriveOS, including NVIDIA DRIVE AGX Thor and MediaTek Dimensity configurations covered by this documentation set. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA. '
            : '. ')
         + 'Select an entry under <strong>Embedded platforms</strong> in Step 2 for eligibility details, or refer to '
         + '<a href="../api/migration/tensorrt-10x-to-11x-driveos.html" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">Migrating TensorRT from 10.x to 11.x on DriveOS</a>.'
         + '</p></div>';
   }

         { platform: 'Rocky Linux 8 x86-64', compiler: 'gcc 11.2.1', glibc: '2.28', python: '3.8' },
         { platform: 'Rocky Linux 9 x86-64', compiler: 'gcc 11.2.1', glibc: '2.28', python: '3.9' },
         { platform: 'SLES 15 x86-64', compiler: 'gcc 11.2.1', glibc: '2.28', python: 'N/A' },
         { platform: 'Windows 10 x64', compiler: 'MSVC 2019 v16.9.2', glibc: 'N/A', python: 'N/A' },
         { platform: 'Windows 11 x64', compiler: 'MSVC 2019 v16.9.2', glibc: 'N/A', python: 'N/A' },
         { platform: 'Windows Server 2022 x64', compiler: 'MSVC 2019 v16.9.2', glibc: 'N/A', python: 'N/A' },
         { platform: 'Ubuntu 24.04 SBSA', compiler: 'gcc 11.4', glibc: '2.35', python: '3.12' },
         { platform: 'NVIDIA JetPack AArch64', compiler: 'gcc 11.4', glibc: '2.35', python: '3.12' }
      ],
      driverRequirements: {
         'CUDA 12.x': { linux: 'R535', windows: 'R537' }
      }
   };

   // Namespace
   window.TensorRTSupport = window.TensorRTSupport || {};

   // Table full view modal functions
   window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModal = function(tableHtml, title) {
      // Create modal if it doesn't exist
      var modal = document.getElementById('nv-table-modal');
      if (!modal) {
         modal = document.createElement('div');
         modal.id = 'nv-table-modal';
         modal.className = 'nv-table-modal';
         modal.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-table-modal-content"><div class="nv-table-modal-header"><h3 class="nv-table-modal-title"></h3><button class="nv-table-modal-close" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.closeTableModal()">&times;</button></div><div class="nv-table-modal-body"></div></div>';
         document.body.appendChild(modal);

         // Close on background click
         modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (e.target === modal) {
               window.TensorRTSupport.closeTableModal();
            }
         });

         // Close on Escape key
         document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.classList.contains('active')) {
               window.TensorRTSupport.closeTableModal();
            }
         });
      }

      modal.querySelector('.nv-table-modal-title').textContent = title || 'Full Table View';
      modal.querySelector('.nv-table-modal-body').innerHTML = tableHtml;
      modal.classList.add('active');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
   };

   window.TensorRTSupport.closeTableModal = function() {
      var modal = document.getElementById('nv-table-modal');
      if (modal) {
         modal.classList.remove('active');
         document.body.style.overflow = '';
      }
   };

   window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll = function(containerId) {
      var container = document.getElementById(containerId);
      if (!container) return;

      var scrollContainer = container.querySelector('div[style*="overflow-x:auto"]');
      if (!scrollContainer) return;

      // Check for both button classes (nv-expand-btn for Explorer 2, nv-table-fullview-btn for others)
      var button = container.querySelector('.nv-expand-btn') || container.querySelector('.nv-table-fullview-btn');
      if (!button) return;

      // Check if scrolling is needed
      var needsScroll = scrollContainer.scrollWidth > scrollContainer.clientWidth;

      if (needsScroll) {
         button.classList.add('visible');
      } else {
         button.classList.remove('visible');
      }
   };

   // Store table HTML for modal viewing
   window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore = window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore || {};

   window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById = function(tableId) {
      var tableHtml = window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId];
      var title = window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore && window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId] || 'Full Table View';
      if (tableHtml) {
         window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModal(tableHtml, title);
      }
   };

   window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore = window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore || {};

   // Toggle important info
   function toggleImportantInfo(toggle, contentId) {
      toggle.classList.toggle('active');
      var content = document.getElementById(contentId);
      if (content) {
         content.classList.toggle('active');
         toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', content.classList.contains('active') ? 'true' : 'false');
      }
   }

   window.toggleImportantInfo = toggleImportantInfo;

   // Helper functions (accessible to all functions)
   function getPlatformKey(platform, versionData) {
      if (platform.includes('Arm64') || platform.includes('Windows on ARM')) return 'Windows on ARM';
      if (platform.includes('Windows')) return 'Windows x64';
      if (platform.includes('DRIVE OS') || platform.includes('(DriveOS)')) return 'NVIDIA DriveOS';
      if (platform.includes('SBSA') || platform.includes('JetPack')) {
         if (versionData && versionData.platformSoftware && versionData.platformSoftware[0]['Linux SBSA + NVIDIA JetPack'] !== undefined) {
            return 'Linux SBSA + NVIDIA JetPack';
         }
         if (platform.includes('JetPack')) return 'NVIDIA JetPack';
         return 'Linux SBSA';
      }
      if (platform.includes('x86-64') || platform.includes('Linux')) return 'Linux x86-64';
      return platform;
   }

   function getPlatformGroup(platform) {
      if (platform.includes('Arm64') || platform.includes('Windows on ARM')) return 'Windows on ARM';
      if (platform.includes('Windows')) return 'Windows';
      if (platform.includes('SBSA')) return 'SBSA';
      if (platform.includes('JetPack')) return 'JetPack';
      if (platform.includes('x86-64') || platform.includes('Linux')) return 'Linux x86-64';
      return platform;
   }

   function getGpuArchitectureFilterKey(item) {
      return item.architectureKey || item.computeCapability;
   }

   function platformSoftwareValuesEqual(a, b) {
      return JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
   }

   function getPlatformSoftwareSummaryGroups(item) {
      var groups = [];
      if (item['Linux x86-64'] !== undefined && item['Windows x64'] !== undefined &&
          platformSoftwareValuesEqual(item['Linux x86-64'], item['Windows x64'])) {
         groups.push({ label: 'Windows and Linux', key: 'Linux x86-64' });
      } else {
         if (item['Linux x86-64'] !== undefined) groups.push({ label: 'Linux x86-64', key: 'Linux x86-64' });
         if (item['Windows x64'] !== undefined) groups.push({ label: 'Windows x64', key: 'Windows x64' });
      }
      ['Linux SBSA', 'NVIDIA JetPack', 'NVIDIA DriveOS', 'Linux SBSA + NVIDIA JetPack', 'Windows on ARM'].forEach(function(key) {
         if (item[key] !== undefined) {
            groups.push({ label: key, key: key });
         }
      });
      return groups;
   }

   function renderCudaToolkitFootnoteBadge(item, platformKey, version) {
      if (!item.footnotes || !item.footnotes[platformKey]) {
         return '';
      }
      var notes = item.footnotes[platformKey];
      var isCuda13Family = version === '13.x' || (version.startsWith('13.') && version !== '13.2');
      var isCuda12Legacy = version.startsWith('12.') && !version.startsWith('12.9');
      // f10: CUDA 13.4 build baseline (TensorRT 11.3.0)
      if (notes.includes('f10') && isCuda13Family) {
         return '<a href="#f10" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote F10">10</a>';
      }
      // f1: CUDA 12.9 build baseline for older 12.x listings
      if (notes.includes('f1') && isCuda12Legacy) {
         return '<a href="#f1" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote F1">1</a>';
      }
      return '';
   }

   function renderPlatformSoftwareValue(item, platformKey) {
      var value = item[platformKey];
      var html = '';
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
         html += '<span class="nv-cuda-versions" style="display:inline-flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start;">';
         value.forEach(function(version) {
            html += '<span class="nv-cuda-tag" style="flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap;margin:0 !important;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">';
            if (version === '12.x' || version === '13.x') {
               html += version;
               html += renderCudaToolkitFootnoteBadge(item, platformKey, version);
            } else {
               html += '<a href="https://developer.nvidia.com/cuda-toolkit-archive" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none;color:inherit;">' + version + '</a>';
               html += renderCudaToolkitFootnoteBadge(item, platformKey, version);
            }
            html += '</span>';
         });
         html += '</span>';
      } else if (value === 'Supported') {
         html += '<span class="nv-card-badge supported">Supported</span>';
      } else if (value === 'N/A') {
         html += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
      } else if (typeof value === 'string' && item.component === 'NVIDIA cuDNN') {
         html += '<span class="nv-cudnn-tag">';
         html += '<a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/cudnn/archives/cudnn-897/release-notes/index.html#rel-897" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none;color:inherit;">' + value + '</a>';
         html += '</span>';
      } else if (typeof value === 'string' && item.component === 'NVIDIA NCCL') {
         html += '<a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/nccl/release-notes/index.html" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + value + '</a>';
      } else if (value !== undefined) {
         html += value;
      }
      return html;
   }

   function renderAggregatedSupportedVersions(item) {
      var groups = getPlatformSoftwareSummaryGroups(item);
      if (groups.length <= 1) {
         return renderPlatformSoftwareValue(item, groups[0] ? groups[0].key : 'Linux x86-64');
      }
      var html = '<ul style="margin:0;padding-left:20px;list-style:disc;">';
      groups.forEach(function(group) {
         html += '<li style="margin:4px 0;line-height:1.6;"><strong>' + group.label + ':</strong> ';
         html += renderPlatformSoftwareValue(item, group.key);
         html += '</li>';
      });
      html += '</ul>';
      return html;
   }

   function cudaValueSupportsMajor(value, major) {
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) return false;
      var prefix = major + '.';
      return value.some(function(v) {
         return v === prefix + 'x' || v.indexOf(prefix) === 0;
      });
   }

   function getSupportedCudaMajorVersions(versionData, platformKey) {
      var cudaItem = null;
      if (versionData && versionData.platformSoftware) {
         versionData.platformSoftware.forEach(function(item) {
            if (item.component === 'NVIDIA CUDA Toolkit') cudaItem = item;
         });
      }
      if (!cudaItem) {
         return { supports12: true, supports13: true };
      }
      if (!platformKey) {
         var supports12 = false;
         var supports13 = false;
         Object.keys(cudaItem).forEach(function(key) {
            if (key === 'component' || key === 'status' || key === 'footnotes') return;
            var value = cudaItem[key];
            if (cudaValueSupportsMajor(value, '12')) supports12 = true;
            if (cudaValueSupportsMajor(value, '13')) supports13 = true;
         });
         return { supports12: supports12, supports13: supports13 };
      }
      var platformValue = cudaItem[platformKey];
      return {
         supports12: cudaValueSupportsMajor(platformValue, '12'),
         supports13: cudaValueSupportsMajor(platformValue, '13')
      };
   }

   function getDriverRowLabel(supports12, supports13) {
      if (supports12 && supports13) {
         return 'NVIDIA Driver (CUDA 12.x or 13.x)';
      }
      return 'NVIDIA Driver';
   }

   function renderDriverTagsHtml(driverInfo) {
      if (!driverInfo) return '';
      if (driverInfo.linux && driverInfo.windows && driverInfo.linux !== driverInfo.windows) {
         return '<span class="nv-driver-tag">' + driverInfo.linux + '</span> <span class="nv-data-table__muted">or later (Linux)</span> <span class="nv-driver-tag">' + driverInfo.windows + '</span> <span class="nv-data-table__muted">or later (Windows)</span>';
      }
      if (driverInfo.linux) {
         return '<span class="nv-driver-tag">' + driverInfo.linux + '</span> <span class="nv-data-table__muted">or later (Linux and Windows)</span>';
      }
      return '';
   }

   function renderDriverSectionHtml(versionData, platformKey) {
      if (!versionData.driverRequirements) return '';
      var cudaMajors = getSupportedCudaMajorVersions(versionData, platformKey);
      var cuda12 = cudaMajors.supports12 ? versionData.driverRequirements['CUDA 12.x'] : null;
      var cuda13 = cudaMajors.supports13 ? versionData.driverRequirements['CUDA 13.x'] : null;
      if (!cuda12 && !cuda13) return '';

      var html = '<tr>';
      html += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + getDriverRowLabel(cudaMajors.supports12, cudaMajors.supports13) + '<a href="#f7" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote F7">7</a> <br><span class="nv-status-badge required">Required</span></td>';
      html += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';

      if (cuda12 && cuda13) {
         html += '<div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong class="nv-data-table__subhead">For CUDA 12.x:</strong> ' + renderDriverTagsHtml(cuda12) + '</div>';
         html += '<div><strong class="nv-data-table__subhead">For CUDA 13.x:</strong> ' + renderDriverTagsHtml(cuda13) + '</div>';
      } else if (cuda12) {
         html += renderDriverTagsHtml(cuda12);
      } else if (cuda13) {
         html += renderDriverTagsHtml(cuda13);
      }

      html += '</td></tr>';
      return html;
   }

   // Consistent platform sorting function - matches dropdown order exactly
   function sortPlatforms(platforms) {
      // Define exact order matching the dropdown
      var platformOrder = {
         // Linux x86-64 group
         'Ubuntu 22.04 x86-64': 1,
         'Ubuntu 24.04 x86-64': 2,
         'RHEL 8 x86-64': 3,
         'RHEL 9 x86-64': 4,
         'RHEL 10 x86-64': 5,
         'Rocky Linux 8 x86-64': 6,
         'Rocky Linux 9 x86-64': 7,
         'Rocky Linux 10 x86-64': 8,
         'Debian 12 x86-64': 9,
         'SLES 15 x86-64': 10,
         // Windows x64 group
         'Windows 10 x64': 10,
         'Windows 11 x64': 11,
         'Windows Server 2022 x64': 12,
         // Windows on ARM group
         'Windows 11 Arm64': 15,
         // Linux SBSA group
         'Ubuntu 24.04 SBSA': 20,
         'Ubuntu 26.04 SBSA': 21,
         'Debian 12 SBSA': 22,
         // NVIDIA JetPack group
         'NVIDIA JetPack AArch64': 30,
         // NVIDIA DriveOS group (11.1.0)
         'Ubuntu 24.04 x86-64 (DriveOS)': 30,
         'DRIVE OS 7 Linux': 31,
         'DRIVE OS 7 QNX': 32,
         // Embedded platforms (TensorRT 11.x)
         'DRIVE Thor (DriveOS 7.2.6+)': 40,
         'DRIVE Orin (DriveOS 7.0)': 41,
         'DRIVE Orin (DriveOS 7.3)': 42,
         'Jetson Orin/Thor (JetPack 7.x)': 43,
         'Jetson Orin (JetPack 6.x)': 44
      };

      return platforms.slice().sort(function(a, b) {
         var aOrder = platformOrder[a] || 999;
         var bOrder = platformOrder[b] || 999;

         // If both have defined order, use that
         if (aOrder !== 999 && bOrder !== 999) {
            return aOrder - bOrder;
         }

         // If only one has defined order, prioritize it
         if (aOrder !== 999) return -1;
         if (bOrder !== 999) return 1;

         // If neither has defined order, sort alphabetically
         return a.localeCompare(b);
      });
   }

   // Dynamic filter updates for Platform and Software Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.updatePlatformSoftwareFilters = function() {
      var platformFilterEl = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
      if (!platformFilterEl) return;
      var platformFilter = platformFilterEl.value;
      var versionFilterEl = document.getElementById('platformVersionFilter');
      var versionFilter = versionFilterEl ? versionFilterEl.value : '';
      var versionData = getVersionData(versionFilter);

      // Calculate available platforms
      var availablePlatforms = new Set();
      var allPlatforms = versionData ? getPlatformListForVersion(versionData) : [];

      if (!platformFilter || platformFilter === 'all') {
         allPlatforms.forEach(function(p) { availablePlatforms.add(p); });
      } else {
         availablePlatforms.add(platformFilter);
      }

      // Update platform filter dropdown
      var platformSelect = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
      if (platformSelect) {
         var currentValue = platformSelect.value;
         // Handle optgroups
         Array.from(platformSelect.querySelectorAll('optgroup')).forEach(function(optgroup) {
            var hasAvailable = false;
            Array.from(optgroup.options).forEach(function(option) {
               if (availablePlatforms.has(option.value)) {
                  option.style.display = '';
                  option.disabled = false;
                  hasAvailable = true;
               } else {
                  option.style.display = 'none';
                  option.disabled = true;
                  if (option.value === currentValue) {
                     platformSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
                  }
               }
            });
            optgroup.style.display = hasAvailable ? '' : 'none';
         });
         // Handle standalone options (like "all")
         Array.from(platformSelect.options).forEach(function(option) {
            if (option.value === '' || option.value === 'all') return;
            if (option.parentElement.tagName !== 'OPTGROUP') {
               if (availablePlatforms.has(option.value)) {
                  option.style.display = '';
                  option.disabled = false;
               } else {
                  option.style.display = 'none';
                  option.disabled = true;
                  if (option.value === currentValue) {
                     platformSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
                  }
               }
            }
         });
      }

   };

   // Dynamic filter updates for GPU Architecture and Precision Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.updateGPUPrecisionFilters = function() {
      var precisionFilter = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionFilter');
      var architectureFilter = document.getElementById('gpuArchitectureFilter');

      if (!precisionFilter || !architectureFilter) return;

      var precisionValue = precisionFilter.value;
      var architectureValue = architectureFilter.value;

      // Get GPU precision data from latest version (11.3.0)
      var latestVersion = '11.3.0';
      var versionData = getVersionData(latestVersion);
      if (!versionData || !versionData.gpuPrecision) return;

      var gpuData = versionData.gpuPrecision;

      // Calculate available architectures
      var availableArchitectures = new Set();
      gpuData.forEach(function(item) {
         availableArchitectures.add(getGpuArchitectureFilterKey(item));
      });

      // Filter by precision
      if (precisionValue && precisionValue !== 'all') {
         availableArchitectures = new Set();
         gpuData.forEach(function(item) {
            if (item[precisionValue] === 'Supported') {
               availableArchitectures.add(getGpuArchitectureFilterKey(item));
            }
         });
      }

      // Update architecture filter dropdown
      var architectureSelect = document.getElementById('gpuArchitectureFilter');
      if (architectureSelect) {
         var currentValue = architectureSelect.value;
         Array.from(architectureSelect.options).forEach(function(option) {
            if (option.value === '' || option.value === 'all') return;
            if (availableArchitectures.has(option.value)) {
               option.style.display = '';
               option.disabled = false;
            } else {
               option.style.display = 'none';
               option.disabled = true;
               if (option.value === currentValue) {
                  architectureSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
               }
            }
         });
      }

      // Calculate available precisions
      var availablePrecisions = new Set(['TF32', 'FP32', 'FP16', 'FP8', 'FP4', 'BF16', 'INT8', 'DLA']);

      // Filter by architecture
      if (architectureValue && architectureValue !== 'all') {
         var gpuItem = gpuData.find(function(item) {
            return getGpuArchitectureFilterKey(item) === architectureValue;
         });
         if (gpuItem) {
            availablePrecisions = new Set();
            ['TF32', 'FP32', 'FP16', 'FP8', 'FP4', 'BF16', 'INT8', 'DLA'].forEach(function(precision) {
               if (gpuItem[precision] === 'Supported') {
                  availablePrecisions.add(precision);
               }
            });
         }
      }

      // Update precision filter dropdown
      var precisionSelect = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionFilter');
      if (precisionSelect) {
         var currentValue = precisionSelect.value;
         Array.from(precisionSelect.options).forEach(function(option) {
            if (option.value === '' || option.value === 'all') return;
            if (availablePrecisions.has(option.value)) {
               option.style.display = '';
               option.disabled = false;
            } else {
               option.style.display = 'none';
               option.disabled = true;
               if (option.value === currentValue) {
                  precisionSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
               }
            }
         });
      }
   };

   // Show/hide platform filter based on version selection
   window.TensorRTSupport.updatePlatformFilterVisibility = function() {
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('platformVersionFilter');
      var platformFilterGroup = document.getElementById('platformFilterGroup');

      if (versionFilter && platformFilterGroup) {
         if (versionFilter.value && versionFilter.value !== '') {
            platformFilterGroup.style.display = 'block';

            var versionData = getVersionData(versionFilter.value);
            var isCombined = versionData && versionData.platformSoftware && versionData.platformSoftware[0]['Linux SBSA + NVIDIA JetPack'] !== undefined;
            var sbsaGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupSBSA');
            var jetpackGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupJetPack');
            var combinedGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupSBSAJetPack');
            var embeddedGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupEmbedded');
            var driveosGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupDriveOS');

            var woaGroup = document.getElementById('optgroupWindowsOnARM');
            if (woaGroup) {
               woaGroup.style.display = versionData && versionData.hideWindowsOnARM ? 'none' : '';
            }
            if (sbsaGroup && jetpackGroup && combinedGroup) {
               if (isCombined) {
                  sbsaGroup.style.display = 'none';
                  jetpackGroup.style.display = 'none';
                  combinedGroup.style.display = '';
               } else {
                  sbsaGroup.style.display = '';
                  jetpackGroup.style.display = versionData && versionData.hideGenericJetPack ? 'none' : '';
                  combinedGroup.style.display = 'none';
               }
            }
            if (embeddedGroup) {
               var platformFilterEl = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
               var showEmbedded = versionFilter.value === '11.2.1' || versionFilter.value === '11.1.0' || versionFilter.value === '11.0.0';
               embeddedGroup.style.display = showEmbedded ? '' : 'none';
               if (!showEmbedded && platformFilterEl && platformFilterEl.value && versionData && versionData.embeddedPlatforms && versionData.embeddedPlatforms[platformFilterEl.value]) {
                  platformFilterEl.selectedIndex = 0;
               }
            }
            if (driveosGroup) {
               var platformFilterElDrive = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
               var showDriveOS = versionFilter.value === '11.2.1' || versionFilter.value === '11.1.0' || versionFilter.value === '11.0.0';
               driveosGroup.style.display = showDriveOS ? '' : 'none';
               if (!showDriveOS && platformFilterElDrive && platformFilterElDrive.value && (platformFilterElDrive.value.indexOf('DRIVE OS') === 0 || platformFilterElDrive.value.indexOf('(DriveOS)') !== -1)) {
                  platformFilterElDrive.selectedIndex = 0;
               }
            }
         } else {
            platformFilterGroup.style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter').value = '';
         }
      }
   };

   // Render Platform and Software Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.renderPlatformSoftware = function() {
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('platformVersionFilter') ? document.getElementById('platformVersionFilter').value : '';
      var platformFilterEl = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
      var platformFilter = platformFilterEl ? platformFilterEl.value : '';
      var grid = document.getElementById('platformSoftwareGrid');

      if (!grid) return;

      var html = '';

      // Check if version is selected first
      if (!versionFilter || versionFilter === '') {
         // No version selected - show prompt
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Please select a TensorRT version above to view platform support information.</div>';
         return;
      }

      // Get version-specific data
      var versionData = getVersionData(versionFilter);
      if (!versionData) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Data not available for TensorRT ' + versionFilter + '. Please select another version.</div>';
         return;
      }

      // Filter logic
      var components = versionData.platformSoftware;
      // Filter out feature components (shown in Explorer 3) and sort: required first, then optional
      components = components.slice().filter(function(item) {
         return item.component !== 'TensorRT Python API' && item.component !== 'ONNX Parser' && item.component !== 'Loops';
      }).sort(function(a, b) {
         var statusOrder = { 'required': 0, 'optional': 1 };
         var aOrder = statusOrder[a.status] !== undefined ? statusOrder[a.status] : 2;
         var bOrder = statusOrder[b.status] !== undefined ? statusOrder[b.status] : 2;
         if (aOrder !== bOrder) return aOrder - bOrder;
         return a.component.localeCompare(b.component);
      });

      // Use all available platforms from compilerPython data, sorted for consistency
      var allPlatforms = getPlatformListForVersion(versionData);
      var platforms = allPlatforms;
      if (platformFilter && platformFilter !== 'all') {
         platforms = [platformFilter];
      }

      // Render based on filters
      if (platformFilter && platformFilter !== '') {
            // Embedded platforms (DriveOS / Jetson) use eligibility view instead of component table
            if (versionData.embeddedPlatforms && versionData.embeddedPlatforms[platformFilter]) {
               grid.innerHTML = renderEmbeddedPlatformView(platformFilter, versionData);
               return;
            }

            // Show specific platform in table format
            var platformKey = getPlatformKey(platformFilter, versionData);
            var tableContainerId = 'platform-software-table-container';
            var tableHtml = '';

            // Build table
            tableHtml += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--wide">';
            tableHtml += '<thead>';
            tableHtml += '<tr>';
            tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Component</th>';
            tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Supported Versions</th>';
            tableHtml += '</tr>';
            tableHtml += '</thead>';
            tableHtml += '<tbody>';

            var rowIndex = 0;
            var driverRowsInserted = false;

            // Render components grouped: required first, then remaining
            components.forEach(function(item) {
               // Insert Driver rows after the last required component
               if (!driverRowsInserted && item.status !== 'required') {
                  driverRowsInserted = true;
                  var driverRowHtml = renderDriverSectionHtml(versionData, platformKey);
                  if (driverRowHtml) {
                     tableHtml += driverRowHtml;
                     rowIndex++;
                  }
               }

               var value = item[platformKey];

               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               var componentLabel = item.component;
               if (item.status === 'required') {
                  componentLabel += '<br><span class="nv-status-badge required">Required</span>';
               }
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + componentLabel + '</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';

               if (Array.isArray(value)) {
                  tableHtml += '<div class="nv-cuda-versions" style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start;">';
                  value.forEach(function(version) {
                     tableHtml += '<span class="nv-cuda-tag" style="flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap;margin:0 !important;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">';

                     // Handle "12.x" and "13.x" as category labels (no archive link); build footnotes still attach
                     if (version === '12.x' || version === '13.x') {
                        tableHtml += version;
                        tableHtml += renderCudaToolkitFootnoteBadge(item, platformKey, version);
                     } else {
                        // Specific version - link to archive
                        tableHtml += '<a href="https://developer.nvidia.com/cuda-toolkit-archive" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none;color:inherit;">' + version + '</a>';
                        tableHtml += renderCudaToolkitFootnoteBadge(item, platformKey, version);
                     }

                     tableHtml += '</span>';
                  });
                  tableHtml += '</div>';
               } else if (value === 'Supported') {
                  tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge supported">Supported</span>';
               } else if (value === 'N/A') {
                  tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
               } else if (typeof value === 'string' && item.component === 'NVIDIA cuDNN') {
                  tableHtml += '<span class="nv-cudnn-tag">';
                  tableHtml += '<a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/cudnn/archives/cudnn-897/release-notes/index.html#rel-897" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none;color:inherit;">' + value + '</a>';
                  tableHtml += '</span>';
               } else if (typeof value === 'string' && item.component === 'NVIDIA NCCL') {
                  tableHtml += '<a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/nccl/release-notes/index.html" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + value + '</a>';
               } else {
                  tableHtml += value;
               }

               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;
            });

            // Fallback: insert Driver rows if not yet inserted (all components were required)
            if (!driverRowsInserted) {
               var fallbackDriverHtml = renderDriverSectionHtml(versionData, platformKey);
               if (fallbackDriverHtml) {
                  driverRowsInserted = true;
                  tableHtml += fallbackDriverHtml;
                  rowIndex++;
               }
            }

            // Add Framework Compatibility (not applicable for Windows on ARM DLA-only)
            var compat = frameworkCompatibility[versionFilter];
            if (compat && platformKey !== 'Windows on ARM') {
               var pytorchUrl = compat.pytorch.startsWith('>=') ? 'v2.0.0' : 'v' + compat.pytorch;

               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">TensorFlow</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml += '<a href="https://github.com/tensorflow/tensorflow/releases/tag/v' + compat.tensorflow + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + compat.tensorflow + '</a>';
               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;

               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">PyTorch</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:4px;"><strong>Tested with:</strong> <a href="https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/releases/tag/' + pytorchUrl + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">PyTorch ' + compat.pytorch + '</a></div>';
               tableHtml += '<div class="nv-data-table__muted"><strong>Compatibility:</strong> May work with older versions.</div>';
               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;

               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">ONNX</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:4px;"><strong>ONNX version:</strong> <a href="https://github.com/onnx/onnx/releases/tag/v' + compat.onnx + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + compat.onnx + '</a></div>';
               // Opset is per-release; fall back to '25' for legacy entries that don't carry an `opset` field yet
               var opsetValue = compat.opset || '25';
               tableHtml += '<div class="nv-data-table__muted" style="margin-bottom:4px;"><strong>Opset support:</strong> Up to opset ' + opsetValue + '</div>';
               tableHtml += '<div class="nv-data-table__muted"><strong>Backward compatibility:</strong> Official support is provided for opset 9 and above</div>';
               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;
            }

            // Add ONNX Operator Support (always shown, not dependent on framework compatibility)
            if (platformKey !== 'Windows on ARM') {
            tableHtml += '<tr>';
            tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">ONNX Operator Support</td>';
            tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
            tableHtml += '<a href="https://github.com/onnx/onnx-tensorrt/blob/master/docs/operators.md" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">View operator support documentation</a>';
            tableHtml += '</td>';
            tableHtml += '</tr>';
            rowIndex++;
            }

            // Compiler and Python Requirements
            var compilerInfo = versionData.compilerPython.filter(function(item) {
               return item.platform === platformFilter;
            });
            var hasCompilerInfo = compilerInfo.length > 0;

            if (hasCompilerInfo) {
                  compilerInfo.forEach(function(item) {
                     if (item.compiler) {
                                 tableHtml += '<tr>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Compiler</td>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
                        if (item.compiler === 'N/A') {
                           tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
                        } else if (item.compilerUrl) {
                           tableHtml += '<a href="' + item.compilerUrl + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + item.compiler + '</a>';
                        } else if (item.compiler.includes('gcc')) {
                           // gcc fallback URLs are version-specific:
                           //   gcc 11.4 ships as the Ubuntu jammy-updates aarch64 cross-toolchain (used by SBSA / JetPack rows)
                           //   gcc 11.2.1 (and other 11.x) ships through the Red Hat GCC Toolset 11 (used by x86-64 RPM/DEB rows)
                           var gccUrl = item.compiler.includes('11.4')
                              ? 'https://packages.ubuntu.com/jammy-updates/g++-11-aarch64-linux-gnu'
                              : item.compiler.includes('13.3')
                              ? 'https://packages.ubuntu.com/noble/g++-13'
                              : 'https://docs.redhat.com/en/documentation/red_hat_enterprise_linux/8/html/developing_c_and_cpp_applications_in_rhel_8/additional-toolsets-for-development_developing-applications#gcc-toolset-11_assembly_additional-toolsets-for-development';
                           tableHtml += '<a href="' + gccUrl + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + item.compiler + '</a>';
                        } else if (item.compiler.includes('MSVC')) {
                           // MSVC fallback URLs branch on toolchain era:
                           //   MSVC 2022 v17.12.13 (used by 11.0.0+ rows that omit compilerUrl) points at the Visual Studio 2022 v17.12 release notes
                           //   MSVC 2019 v16.9.2 (used by all 10.x and older rows) points at the Visual Studio 2019 v16.9 release notes
                           var msvcUrl = item.compiler.includes('2022')
                              ? 'https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/visualstudio/releases/2022/release-notes-v17.12#17.12.13'
                              : 'https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/visualstudio/releases/2019/release-notes-v16.9#--visual-studio-2019-version-1692';
                           tableHtml += '<a href="' + msvcUrl + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + item.compiler + '</a>';
                        } else {
                           tableHtml += '<span style="font-weight:500;">' + item.compiler + '</span>';
                        }
                        tableHtml += '</td>';
                        tableHtml += '</tr>';
                        rowIndex++;
                     }

                     if (item.glibc) {
                                 tableHtml += '<tr>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Minimum GNU C Library (glibc) Version</td>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
                        if (item.glibc === 'N/A') {
                           tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
                        } else {
                           tableHtml += '<span style="font-weight:500;">' + item.glibc + '</span>';
                        }
                        tableHtml += '</td>';
                        tableHtml += '</tr>';
                        rowIndex++;
                     }

                     if (item.python) {
                                 tableHtml += '<tr>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Python <a href="#f6" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote F6">6</a></td>';
                        tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
                        if (item.python === 'N/A') {
                           tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
                        } else {
                           // Convert "3.10" to "3100" for Python.org URL format
                           var pythonUrl = item.python.replace(/\./g, '') + '0';
                           tableHtml += '<a href="https://www.python.org/downloads/release/python-' + pythonUrl + '/" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + item.python + '</a>';
                        }
                        tableHtml += '</td>';
                        tableHtml += '</tr>';
                        rowIndex++;
                     }
                  });
            }

            // Supported Compute Capabilities
            // Match exact platform name (Windows platforms have separate entries)
            var matchingCap = versionData.computeCapabilities.find(function(item) {
               // For Windows on ARM, match exact name
               if (platformFilter === 'Windows 11 Arm64' || platformFilter.includes('Windows on ARM')) {
                  return item.platform === platformFilter;
               }
               // For Windows x64 platforms, match exact name
               if (platformFilter === 'Windows 10 x64' || platformFilter === 'Windows 11 x64' || platformFilter === 'Windows Server 2022 x64') {
                  return item.platform === platformFilter;
               }
               // For SBSA platforms, match exact name
               else if (platformFilter.includes('SBSA')) {
                  return item.platform === platformFilter;
               }
               // For DriveOS host/target platforms, match exact name
               else if (platformFilter.includes('(DriveOS)') || platformFilter.includes('DRIVE OS')) {
                  return item.platform === platformFilter;
               }
               // For Linux x86-64 platforms, use generic 'Linux x86-64'
               else if (platformFilter.includes('x86-64') || (platformFilter.includes('Linux') && !platformFilter.includes('SBSA'))) {
                  return item.platform === 'Linux x86-64';
               }
               // For other platforms (like JetPack), match exact name
               else {
                  return item.platform === platformFilter;
               }
            });

            if (matchingCap) {
               var capabilities = matchingCap.capabilities;
               var capsHtml = '';

               capabilities.forEach(function(cap) {
                  capsHtml += '<span class="nv-compute-cap" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:6px;">';
                  capsHtml += cap;
                  if (matchingCap.footnotes && matchingCap.footnotes[cap]) {
                     var footnoteId = matchingCap.footnotes[cap];
                     var fnUpper = footnoteId.toUpperCase();
                     capsHtml += '<a href="#' + footnoteId + '" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote ' + fnUpper + '">' + fnUpper.replace('F', '') + '</a>';
                  }
                  capsHtml += '</span>';
               });

               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">Supported Compute Capabilities</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml += '<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;">' + capsHtml + '</div>';
               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
            }

            tableHtml += '</tbody>';
            tableHtml += '</table>';

            // Store table HTML for modal
            var tableId = 'platform-software-table-' + Date.now();
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId] = tableHtml;
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId] = 'TensorRT ' + versionFilter + ' - ' + platformFilter;

            // Add button and container
            html += '<div id="' + tableContainerId + '">';
            html += '<button class="nv-table-fullview-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + tableId + '\')">View Full Span Table</button>';
            html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
            html += tableHtml;
            html += '</div>';
            html += '</div>';
         } else {
            // Version-only: show component requirements summary (CUDA by platform group) plus supported platforms
            var summaryContainerId = 'platform-software-summary-container';
            var summaryHtml = '';
            summaryHtml += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--wide">';
            summaryHtml += '<thead><tr>';
            summaryHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Component</th>';
            summaryHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Supported Versions</th>';
            summaryHtml += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

            components.forEach(function(item) {
               summaryHtml += '<tr>';
               var componentLabel = item.component;
               if (item.status === 'required') {
                  componentLabel += '<br><span class="nv-status-badge required">Required</span>';
               }
               summaryHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + componentLabel + '</td>';
               summaryHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">' + renderAggregatedSupportedVersions(item) + '</td>';
               summaryHtml += '</tr>';
            });

            summaryHtml += renderDriverSectionHtml(versionData, null);

            summaryHtml += '</tbody></table>';

            var summaryTableId = 'platform-software-summary-' + Date.now();
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[summaryTableId] = summaryHtml;
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[summaryTableId] = 'TensorRT ' + versionFilter + ' - Software and System Requirements';

            html += '<div id="' + summaryContainerId + '">';
            html += '<button class="nv-table-fullview-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + summaryTableId + '\')">View Full Span Table</button>';
            html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
            html += summaryHtml;
            html += '</div>';
            html += '</div>';

            if (versionFilter === '11.3.0' || versionFilter === '11.2.1' || versionFilter === '11.1.0' || versionFilter === '11.0.0') {
               html += renderJetPack11xCallout(versionFilter);
            }

            // Supported platform list (select a platform in Step 2 for compiler, Python, and compute-capability details)
            var allPlatforms = getPlatformListForVersion(versionData).filter(function(platform) {
               return !versionData.embeddedPlatforms || !versionData.embeddedPlatforms[platform];
            });

            var tableContainerId = 'platform-list-table-container';
            var tableHtml = '';

            tableHtml += '<h3 class="nv-section-subheading">Supported Platforms</h3>';
            tableHtml += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--compact">';
            tableHtml += '<thead>';
            tableHtml += '<tr>';
            tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Platform</th>';
            tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Status</th>';
            tableHtml += '</tr>';
            tableHtml += '</thead>';
            tableHtml += '<tbody>';

            allPlatforms.forEach(function(platform) {
               tableHtml += '<tr>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + platform + '</td>';
               tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge supported">Supported</span>';
               tableHtml += '</td>';
               tableHtml += '</tr>';
            });

            tableHtml += '</tbody>';
            tableHtml += '</table>';

            var tableId = 'platform-list-table-' + Date.now();
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId] = tableHtml;
            window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId] = 'TensorRT ' + versionFilter + ' - Supported Platforms';

            html += '<div id="' + tableContainerId + '">';
            html += '<button class="nv-table-fullview-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + tableId + '\')">View Full Span Table</button>';
            html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
            html += tableHtml;
            html += '</div>';
            html += '</div>';
         }

      grid.innerHTML = html || '<div class="nv-prompt">No results found for the selected filters.</div>';

      // Check if scrolling is needed after a short delay to ensure rendering is complete
      setTimeout(function() {
         if (platformFilter && platformFilter !== '') {
            window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll('platform-software-table-container');
         } else {
            window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll('platform-software-summary-container');
            window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll('platform-list-table-container');
         }
      }, 100);
   };

   // Reset Platform and Software Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.resetPlatformSoftware = function() {
      // Clear grid FIRST to immediately remove results
      var grid = document.getElementById('platformSoftwareGrid');
      if (grid) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Select a TensorRT version above to view compatibility information. Optionally select a platform to filter results.</div>';
      }

      // Reset filter values
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('platformVersionFilter');
      if (versionFilter) {
         versionFilter.value = '';
         versionFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      var platformFilter = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
      if (platformFilter) {
         platformFilter.value = '';
         platformFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      // Hide platform filter
      var platformFilterGroup = document.getElementById('platformFilterGroup');
      if (platformFilterGroup) platformFilterGroup.style.display = 'none';

      // Show all filter options again
      var componentSelect = document.getElementById('platformComponentFilter');
      if (componentSelect) {
         Array.from(componentSelect.options).forEach(function(option) {
            option.style.display = '';
            option.disabled = false;
         });
      }

      if (platformFilter) {
         Array.from(platformFilter.querySelectorAll('optgroup')).forEach(function(optgroup) {
            optgroup.style.display = '';
            Array.from(optgroup.options).forEach(function(option) {
               option.style.display = '';
               option.disabled = false;
            });
         });
         Array.from(platformFilter.options).forEach(function(option) {
            if (option.parentElement.tagName !== 'OPTGROUP') {
               option.style.display = '';
               option.disabled = false;
            }
         });
      }
   };

   // Render GPU Architecture and Precision Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.renderGPUPrecision = function() {
      var precisionFilter = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionFilter');
      var architectureFilter = document.getElementById('gpuArchitectureFilter');
      var grid = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionGrid');

      if (!grid || !precisionFilter || !architectureFilter) return;

      var precisionValue = precisionFilter.value;
      var architectureValue = architectureFilter.value;
      var html = '';

      // Get GPU precision data from latest version (11.3.0) or aggregate from all versions
      // Since GPU precision data is typically the same across versions, use latest
      var latestVersion = '11.3.0';
      var versionData = getVersionData(latestVersion);
      if (!versionData || !versionData.gpuPrecision) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">GPU precision data not available.</div>';
         return;
      }

      var gpus = versionData.gpuPrecision.slice(); // Make a copy

      if (architectureValue && architectureValue !== 'all') {
         gpus = gpus.filter(function(item) { return getGpuArchitectureFilterKey(item) === architectureValue; });
      }

      if (precisionValue && precisionValue !== 'all') {
         gpus = gpus.filter(function(item) { return item[precisionValue] === 'Supported'; });
      }


      // Mapping from compute capability to dropdown display format
      var architectureDisplayNames = {
         '12.1': '12.1 (DGX Spark)',
         '12.0': '12.0 (RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell)',
         '11.0': '11.0 (Jetson AGX Thor)',
         '10.7': '10.7 (NVIDIA Vera Rubin)',
         '10.3': '10.3 (B300)',
         '10.0': '10.0 (B200)',
         '9.0': '9.0 (H100, GH200)',
         '8.9': '8.9 (L40S)',
         '8.7': '8.7 (Jetson AGX Orin)',
         '8.6': '8.6 (A10)',
         '8.0': '8.0 (A100)',
         '7.5': '7.5 (T4)'
      };

      // Build table format
      var tableContainerId = 'gpu-precision-table-container';
      var tableHtml = '';
      tableHtml += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--gpu">';
      tableHtml += '<thead>';
      tableHtml += '<tr>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">GPU Architecture</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">TF32</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">FP32</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">FP16</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">FP8</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">FP4</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">BF16</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">INT8</th>';
      tableHtml += '<th class="nv-data-table__th nv-data-table__th--center">DLA</th>';
      tableHtml += '</tr>';
      tableHtml += '</thead>';
      tableHtml += '<tbody>';

      var rowIndex = 0;
      gpus.forEach(function(item) {
         var displayName = item.architectureLabel || architectureDisplayNames[item.computeCapability] || item.computeCapability;

                                 tableHtml += '<tr>';
         tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + displayName + '</td>';

         // Precision columns
         ['TF32', 'FP32', 'FP16', 'FP8', 'FP4', 'BF16', 'INT8', 'DLA'].forEach(function(precision) {
            if (precisionValue && precisionValue !== 'all' && precision !== precisionValue) {
               // Skip this precision if filtered, but still need to add empty cell for table structure
               // Actually, if filtering by precision, we should only show that column
               return;
            }

            var value = item[precision];
            tableHtml += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--center">';
            if (value === 'Supported') {
               tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge supported" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">Supported';
               // Add footnote badge if present
               if (item.footnotes && item.footnotes[precision]) {
                  var footnoteId = item.footnotes[precision];
                  var fnUpper = footnoteId.toUpperCase();
                  var fnNumber = fnUpper.replace('F', '');
                  tableHtml += '<a href="#' + footnoteId + '" class="nv-footnote-badge" title="Click to view footnote ' + fnUpper + '">' + fnNumber + '</a>';
               }
               tableHtml += '</span>';
            } else if (value === 'N/A') {
               tableHtml += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">N/A</span>';
            } else {
               tableHtml += value || '-';
            }
            tableHtml += '</td>';
         });

         tableHtml += '</tr>';
         rowIndex++;
      });

      tableHtml += '</tbody>';
      tableHtml += '</table>';

      // Store table HTML for modal
      var tableId = 'gpu-precision-table-' + Date.now();
      window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId] = tableHtml;
      window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId] = 'GPU Precision Support';

      // Add button and container
      html += '<div id="' + tableContainerId + '">';
      html += '<button class="nv-expand-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + tableId + '\')">&#x26F6; Open table in full-screen view</button>';
      html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
      html += tableHtml;
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';

      grid.innerHTML = html || '<div class="nv-prompt">No results found for the selected filters.</div>';

      // Check if scrolling is needed after a short delay to ensure rendering is complete
      setTimeout(function() {
         window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll(tableContainerId);
      }, 100);
   };

   // Feature Component Support Data (version-independent)
   // Documentation links mapping
   var componentDocLinks = {
      'TensorRT Python API': '../inference-library/python-api-docs.html',
      'ONNX Parser': '../inference-library/advanced.html#onnx-parser',
      'Loops': '../inference-library/work-with-loops.html',
      'Conditionals': '../inference-library/work-with-conditionals.html',
      'Dynamic Shapes': '../inference-library/work-dynamic-shapes.html',
      'DLA': '../inference-library/work-with-dla.html',
      'Weight Streaming': '../inference-library/advanced.html#weight-streaming',
      'INT4 Weight Only Quantization': '../inference-library/work-with-quantized-types.html',
      'FP4 Data Type': '../inference-library/work-with-quantized-types.html',
      'API Capture and Replay': '../inference-library/capture-replay.html',
      'MoE': '../inference-library/work-with-transformers.html#mixture-of-experts',
      'Multi-Device Inference': '../inference-library/multi-device-inference.html',
      'Global Performance Tuner': '../performance/tuning.html'
   };

   // Helper function to get color for TensorRT version pill
   function getTensorRTVersionColor(version) {
      // Color code by major.minor version
      // Latest versions: Green gradient (darker = newer)
      if (version.startsWith('10.16')) return '#2E7D32'; // Dark green (latest)
      if (version.startsWith('10.15')) return '#388E3C'; // Green
      if (version.startsWith('10.14')) return '#43A047'; // Light green
      if (version.startsWith('10.13')) return '#66BB6A'; // Lighter green
      if (version.startsWith('10.12')) return '#81C784'; // Light green
      if (version.startsWith('10.11')) return '#A5D6A7'; // Very light green
      if (version.startsWith('10.10')) return '#C8E6C9'; // Pale green
      if (version.startsWith('10.9')) return '#E8F5E9'; // Very pale green
      // Mid versions: Blue gradient
      if (version.startsWith('10.8')) return '#E3F2FD'; // Light blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.7')) return '#BBDEFB'; // Blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.6')) return '#90CAF9'; // Medium blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.5')) return '#64B5F6'; // Darker blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.4')) return '#42A5F5'; // Blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.3')) return '#2196F3'; // Dark blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.2')) return '#1E88E5'; // Darker blue
      if (version.startsWith('10.1')) return '#1976D2'; // Very dark blue
      // Earliest versions: Gray/neutral gradient
      if (version.startsWith('10.0')) return '#9E9E9E'; // Medium gray (earliest)
      return '#BDBDBD'; // Light gray for unknown
   }

   // Helper function to render TensorRT version as pill
   function renderTensorRTVersionPill(version) {
      var color = getTensorRTVersionColor(version);
      // Use white text for darker backgrounds (10.3, 10.2, 10.1, 10.0), dark text for lighter backgrounds
      var textColor = version.startsWith('10.0') || version.startsWith('10.1') || version.startsWith('10.2') || version.startsWith('10.3') ? '#fff' : '#333';
      return '<span class="nv-cuda-tag" style="background-color:' + color + ';color:' + textColor + ';border-color:' + color + ';">' + version + '</span>';
   }

   function getFeatureComponentSupport(version) {
      var features = [
         { component: 'TensorRT Python API', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Available in all TensorRT 10.x versions', versionNotes: { '11.0.0': 'Python 3.8 and 3.9 bindings are deprecated and will be removed in a future release', '11.1.0': 'Python 3.8 and 3.9 bindings are deprecated and will be removed in a future release; Python 3.14 bindings are being added in 11.1.0 (preliminary)', '11.2.1': 'Python 3.8 and 3.9 bindings are deprecated and will be removed in a future release; Python samples support 3.13 and 3.14 on supported platforms; Windows Python 3.14 support remains preliminary' } },
         { component: 'ONNX Parser', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Available in all TensorRT 10.x versions' },
         { component: 'Loops', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Available in all TensorRT 10.x versions', versionNotes: { '11.0.0': 'Loops with scan outputs (ILoopOutputLayer with kCONCATENATE or kREVERSE) must now have an ITripLimitLayer with kCOUNT. This requirement is now explicitly enforced instead of silently undefined behavior', '11.1.0': 'Loops with scan outputs (ILoopOutputLayer with kCONCATENATE or kREVERSE) must have an ITripLimitLayer with kCOUNT (same as 11.0.0)' } },
         { component: 'Conditionals', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Available in all TensorRT 10.x versions' },
         { component: 'Dynamic Shapes', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Available in all TensorRT 10.x versions' },
         { component: 'DLA', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', lastSupported: '10.7.0', notes: 'Supported until TensorRT 10.7 when using JetPack 6. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA.', unsupportedNote: 'Not supported in TensorRT 10.8 through 11.3.0, or in TensorRT 11.3.1 for DriveOS. TensorRT 10.7 was the last release that supported DLA.' },
         { component: 'Weight Streaming', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'Preview feature, enhanced in 10.1.0' },
         { component: 'INT4 Weight Only Quantization', introduced: '10.0.0 EA', notes: 'NVIDIA Hopper GPUs (SM 90)' },
         { component: 'FP4 Data Type', introduced: '10.8.0', notes: 'NVIDIA Blackwell architecture GPUs (SM 100+)', versionNotes: { '11.1.0': 'High-precision weights used in FP4 double quantization are not refittable (same as 11.0.0); NVFP4 dual-GEMM fusion for SM121 is being added in 11.1.0 (preliminary)', '11.2.1': 'High-precision weights used in FP4 double quantization are not refittable (same as 11.0.0)', '11.3.0': 'High-precision weights used in FP4 double quantization are refittable' } },
         { component: 'API Capture and Replay', introduced: '10.13.3', notes: 'Enhanced in 10.16.0' },
         { component: 'MoE', introduced: '10.16.0', notes: 'SM110 with NVFP4 + FP8', versionNotes: { '11.0.0': 'Backend perf improvements for the NVIDIA Blackwell architecture (SM10x, SM110); the previous seqLen ≤ 16 guidance no longer applies', '11.1.0': 'Backend perf improvements for the NVIDIA Blackwell architecture (SM10x, SM110) carried from 11.0.0; the previous seqLen ≤ 16 guidance no longer applies' } },
         { component: 'Multi-Device Inference', introduced: '11.0.0', notes: 'Introduced as a preview feature in 10.16.0. Requires SM 80+' },
         { component: 'Global Performance Tuner', introduced: '11.1.0', notes: 'Automated end-to-end performance tuning through trtexec build-route search' }
      ];

      var versionOrder = ['10.0.0 EA', '10.0.1', '10.1.0', '10.2.0', '10.3.0', '10.4.0', '10.5.0', '10.6.0', '10.7.0', '10.8.0', '10.9.0', '10.10.0', '10.11.0', '10.12.0', '10.13.0', '10.13.2', '10.13.3', '10.14.1', '10.15.1', '10.16.0', '10.16.1', '11.0.0', '11.1.0', '11.2.1', '11.3.0'];

      return features.map(function(feature) {
         var introducedIdx = versionOrder.indexOf(feature.introduced);
         var currentIdx = versionOrder.indexOf(version);
         var lastSupportedIdx = feature.lastSupported
            ? versionOrder.indexOf(feature.lastSupported)
            : versionOrder.length - 1;
         var supported = currentIdx >= introducedIdx && currentIdx <= lastSupportedIdx;
         if (!supported && feature.alsoSupported) {
            feature.alsoSupported.forEach(function(window) {
               var fromIdx = versionOrder.indexOf(window.from);
               var toIdx = versionOrder.indexOf(window.to);
               if (currentIdx >= fromIdx && currentIdx <= toIdx) {
                  supported = true;
               }
            });
         }

         var notes = !supported && feature.unsupportedNote
            ? feature.unsupportedNote
            : (feature.versionNotes && feature.versionNotes[version])
               ? feature.versionNotes[version]
               : feature.notes;

         return {
            component: feature.component,
            supported: supported,
            introduced: feature.introduced,
            notes: notes
         };
      });
   }

   // Render Feature Component Support
   window.TensorRTSupport.renderFeatureComponent = function() {
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('featureVersionFilter').value;
      var componentFilter = document.getElementById('featureComponentFilter').value;
      var grid = document.getElementById('featureComponentGrid');

      if (!versionFilter) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Please select a TensorRT version above.</div>';
         return;
      }

      var features = getFeatureComponentSupport(versionFilter);

      // Filter by component if specified
      if (componentFilter && componentFilter !== 'all') {
         features = features.filter(function(f) { return f.component === componentFilter; });
      }

      if (features.length === 0) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">No features found for the selected filters.</div>';
         return;
      }

      var html = '';


      // Group by support status
      var supported = features.filter(function(f) { return f.supported; });
      var notSupported = features.filter(function(f) { return !f.supported; });
      var tableContainerId1, tableContainerId2;

      if (versionFilter === 'all') {
         // Show all versions - table format
         var allVersions = ['10.0.0 EA', '10.0.1', '10.1.0', '10.2.0', '10.3.0', '10.4.0', '10.5.0', '10.6.0', '10.7.0', '10.8.0', '10.9.0', '10.10.0', '10.11.0', '10.12.0', '10.13.0', '10.13.2', '10.13.3', '10.14.1', '10.15.1', '10.16.0', '10.16.1', '11.0.0', '11.1.0', '11.2.1', '11.3.0'];
         var allFeatures = getFeatureComponentSupport('11.3.0').map(function(f) { return f.component; });

         if (componentFilter && componentFilter !== 'all') {
            allFeatures = [componentFilter];
         }

         tableContainerId1 = 'feature-component-table-container-1';
         var tableHtml1 = '';
         tableHtml1 += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--wide">';
         html += '<thead>';
         html += '<tr>';
         tableHtml1 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Component</th>';
         tableHtml1 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Supported Versions</th>';
         tableHtml1 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Introduced</th>';
         tableHtml1 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Notes</th>';
         tableHtml1 += '</tr>';
         tableHtml1 += '</thead>';
         tableHtml1 += '<tbody>';

         var rowIndex = 0;
         allFeatures.forEach(function(componentName) {
            var supportedVersions = [];
            allVersions.forEach(function(v) {
               var feature = getFeatureComponentSupport(v).find(function(f) { return f.component === componentName; });
               if (feature && feature.supported) {
                  supportedVersions.push(v);
               }
            });

            if (supportedVersions.length > 0) {
               var introFeature = getFeatureComponentSupport('11.3.0').find(function(f) { return f.component === componentName; });
               var docLink = componentDocLinks[componentName];
               var componentDisplay = docLink ? '<a href="' + docLink + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + componentName + '</a>' : componentName;

               tableHtml1 += '<tr>';
               tableHtml1 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + componentDisplay + '</td>';
               tableHtml1 += '<td style="padding:12px 16px;vertical-align:top;">';
               tableHtml1 += '<div class="nv-cuda-versions" style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start;align-content:flex-start;max-width:100%;">';
               supportedVersions.forEach(function(v) {
                  tableHtml1 += '<span class="nv-cuda-tag" style="flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap;margin:0 !important;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">' + v + '</span>';
               });
               tableHtml1 += '</div>';
               tableHtml1 += '</td>';
               tableHtml1 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               if (introFeature && introFeature.introduced) {
                  tableHtml1 += renderTensorRTVersionPill(introFeature.introduced);
               } else {
                  tableHtml1 += '<span class="nv-data-table__muted">N/A</span>';
               }
               tableHtml1 += '</td>';
               tableHtml1 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--notes">' + (introFeature && introFeature.notes ? introFeature.notes : '-') + '</td>';
               tableHtml1 += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;
            }
         });

         tableHtml1 += '</tbody>';
         tableHtml1 += '</table>';

         // Store table HTML for modal
         var tableId1 = 'feature-component-table-1-' + Date.now();
         window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId1] = tableHtml1;
         window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId1] = 'Feature Component Support - All Versions';

         // Add button and container
         html += '<div id="' + tableContainerId1 + '">';
         html += '<button class="nv-table-fullview-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + tableId1 + '\')">View Full Span Table</button>';
         html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
         html += tableHtml1;
         html += '</div>';
         html += '</div>';
      } else {
         // Show single version - table format
         tableContainerId2 = 'feature-component-table-container-2';
         var tableHtml2 = '';
         tableHtml2 += '<table class="nv-data-table nv-data-table--wide">';
         html += '<thead>';
         html += '<tr>';
         tableHtml2 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Component</th>';
         tableHtml2 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Status</th>';
         tableHtml2 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Introduced</th>';
         tableHtml2 += '<th class="nv-data-table__th">Notes</th>';
         tableHtml2 += '</tr>';
         tableHtml2 += '</thead>';
         tableHtml2 += '<tbody>';

         var rowIndex = 0;
         supported.forEach(function(feature) {
            var docLink = componentDocLinks[feature.component];
            var componentDisplay = docLink ? '<a href="' + docLink + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link">' + feature.component + '</a>' : feature.component;

            tableHtml2 += '<tr>';
            tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + componentDisplay + '</td>';
            tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
            tableHtml2 += '<span class="nv-card-badge supported">Supported</span>';
            tableHtml2 += '</td>';
            tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
            tableHtml2 += renderTensorRTVersionPill(feature.introduced);
            tableHtml2 += '</td>';
            tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--notes">' + (feature.notes || '-') + '</td>';
            tableHtml2 += '</tr>';
            rowIndex++;
         });

         if (notSupported.length > 0 && !componentFilter) {
            notSupported.forEach(function(feature) {
               var docLink = componentDocLinks[feature.component];
               var componentDisplay = docLink ? '<a href="' + docLink + '" target="_blank" class="nv-matrix-link nv-matrix-link--dim">' + feature.component + '</a>' : feature.component;

               tableHtml2 += '<tr class="nv-data-table__row--dim">';
               tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--label">' + componentDisplay + '</td>';
               tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml2 += '<span class="nv-card-badge na">Not Available</span>';
               tableHtml2 += '</td>';
               tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td">';
               tableHtml2 += renderTensorRTVersionPill(feature.introduced);
               tableHtml2 += '</td>';
               tableHtml2 += '<td class="nv-data-table__td nv-data-table__td--notes">-</td>';
               tableHtml2 += '</tr>';
               rowIndex++;
            });
         }

         tableHtml2 += '</tbody>';
         tableHtml2 += '</table>';

         // Store table HTML for modal
         var tableId2 = 'feature-component-table-2-' + Date.now();
