         window.TensorRTSupport.tableHtmlStore[tableId2] = tableHtml2;
         window.TensorRTSupport.tableTitleStore[tableId2] = 'Feature Component Support - TensorRT ' + versionFilter;

         // Add button and container
         html += '<div id="' + tableContainerId2 + '">';
         html += '<button class="nv-table-fullview-btn" onclick="window.TensorRTSupport.openTableModalById(\'' + tableId2 + '\')">View Full Span Table</button>';
         html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;margin-top:20px;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;">';
         html += tableHtml2;
         html += '</div>';
         html += '</div>';
      }

      grid.innerHTML = html || '<div class="nv-prompt">No results found for the selected filters.</div>';

      // Check if scrolling is needed after a short delay to ensure rendering is complete
      setTimeout(function() {
         if (versionFilter === 'all') {
            window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll(tableContainerId1);
         } else {
            window.TensorRTSupport.checkTableScroll(tableContainerId2);
         }
      }, 100);
   };

   // Update Feature Component Filter Options
   window.TensorRTSupport.updateFeatureComponentFilters = function() {
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('featureVersionFilter').value;
      var componentGroup = document.getElementById('featureComponentFilterGroup');
      var componentFilter = document.getElementById('featureComponentFilter');

      if (versionFilter && versionFilter !== 'all') {
         componentGroup.style.display = 'block';

         // Get supported components for this version
         var features = getFeatureComponentSupport(versionFilter);
         var supportedComponents = features.filter(function(f) { return f.supported; }).map(function(f) { return f.component; });

         // Store current selection
         var currentSelection = componentFilter.value;

         // Clear and rebuild dropdown
         componentFilter.innerHTML = '';

         // Add default option
         var defaultOption = document.createElement('option');
         defaultOption.value = '';
         defaultOption.textContent = '-- Select a component --';
         defaultOption.selected = true;
         componentFilter.appendChild(defaultOption);

         // Add "All Components" option
         var allOption = document.createElement('option');
         allOption.value = 'all';
         allOption.textContent = 'All Components';
         componentFilter.appendChild(allOption);

         // Add only supported components
         supportedComponents.forEach(function(component) {
            var option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = component;
            option.textContent = component;

            // Handle special display names
            if (component === 'DLA') {
               option.textContent = 'DLA (Deep Learning Accelerator)';
            } else if (component === 'MoE') {
               option.textContent = 'MoE (Mixture of Experts)';
            }

            componentFilter.appendChild(option);
         });

         // Restore selection if it's still valid, otherwise clear it
         if (currentSelection && supportedComponents.indexOf(currentSelection) !== -1) {
            componentFilter.value = currentSelection;
         } else {
            componentFilter.value = '';
         }
      } else if (versionFilter === 'all') {
         // For "all versions", show all components
         componentGroup.style.display = 'block';

         // Store current selection
         var currentSelection = componentFilter.value;

         // Rebuild with all components
         componentFilter.innerHTML = '';

         var defaultOption = document.createElement('option');
         defaultOption.value = '';
         defaultOption.textContent = '-- Select a component --';
         defaultOption.selected = true;
         componentFilter.appendChild(defaultOption);

         var allOption = document.createElement('option');
         allOption.value = 'all';
         allOption.textContent = 'All Components';
         componentFilter.appendChild(allOption);

         // Get all components from the latest version
         var allFeatures = getFeatureComponentSupport('11.3.0');
         allFeatures.forEach(function(feature) {
            var option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = feature.component;
            option.textContent = feature.component;

            if (feature.component === 'DLA') {
               option.textContent = 'DLA (Deep Learning Accelerator)';
            } else if (feature.component === 'MoE') {
               option.textContent = 'MoE (Mixture of Experts)';
            }

            componentFilter.appendChild(option);
         });

         // Restore selection if it exists
         if (currentSelection) {
            componentFilter.value = currentSelection;
         } else {
            componentFilter.value = '';
         }
      } else {
         componentGroup.style.display = 'none';
         componentFilter.value = '';
      }
   };

   // Reset Feature Component
   window.TensorRTSupport.resetFeatureComponent = function() {
      // Clear grid FIRST to immediately remove results
      var grid = document.getElementById('featureComponentGrid');
      if (grid) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Select a TensorRT version above to view feature support information. Optionally select a component to filter results.</div>';
      }

      // Reset filter values
      var versionFilter = document.getElementById('featureVersionFilter');
      if (versionFilter) {
         versionFilter.value = '';
         versionFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      var componentFilter = document.getElementById('featureComponentFilter');
      if (componentFilter) {
         componentFilter.value = '';
         componentFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      // Hide component filter group
      var componentGroup = document.getElementById('featureComponentFilterGroup');
      if (componentGroup) {
         componentGroup.style.display = 'none';
      }
   };

   // Reset GPU Precision
   window.TensorRTSupport.resetGPUPrecision = function() {
      // Clear grid FIRST to immediately remove results
      var grid = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionGrid');
      if (grid) {
         grid.innerHTML = '<div class="nv-prompt">Select filters above to display GPU architecture and precision support.</div>';
      }

      // Reset filter values
      var precisionFilter = document.getElementById('gpuPrecisionFilter');
      if (precisionFilter) {
         precisionFilter.value = 'all';
         precisionFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      var architectureFilter = document.getElementById('gpuArchitectureFilter');
      if (architectureFilter) {
         architectureFilter.value = 'all';
         architectureFilter.selectedIndex = 0;
      }

      // Show all filter options again
      if (precisionFilter) {
         Array.from(precisionFilter.options).forEach(function(option) {
            option.style.display = '';
            option.disabled = false;
         });
      }

      if (architectureFilter) {
         Array.from(architectureFilter.options).forEach(function(option) {
            option.style.display = '';
            option.disabled = false;
         });
      }
   };

   // Auto-refresh explorers when any matrix dropdown changes
   function onMatrixFilterChange(event) {
      var target = event.target;
      if (!target || target.tagName !== 'SELECT' || !target.classList.contains('nv-filter-select')) {
         return;
      }

      switch (target.id) {
         case 'platformVersionFilter':
            window.TensorRTSupport.updatePlatformFilterVisibility();
            var platformSelect = document.getElementById('platformPlatformFilter');
            if (platformSelect) {
               platformSelect.value = '';
               platformSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
            }
            window.TensorRTSupport.renderPlatformSoftware();
            break;
         case 'platformPlatformFilter':
            window.TensorRTSupport.updatePlatformSoftwareFilters();
            window.TensorRTSupport.renderPlatformSoftware();
            break;
         case 'gpuPrecisionFilter':
         case 'gpuArchitectureFilter':
            window.TensorRTSupport.updateGPUPrecisionFilters();
            window.TensorRTSupport.renderGPUPrecision();
            break;
         case 'featureVersionFilter':
            window.TensorRTSupport.updateFeatureComponentFilters();
            window.TensorRTSupport.renderFeatureComponent();
            break;
         case 'featureComponentFilter':
            window.TensorRTSupport.renderFeatureComponent();
            break;
      }
   }

   function initMatrixAutoRefresh() {
      if (window.TensorRTSupport._matrixAutoRefreshBound) {
         return;
      }
      document.addEventListener('change', onMatrixFilterChange);
      window.TensorRTSupport._matrixAutoRefreshBound = true;
   }

   function init() {
      initMatrixAutoRefresh();
   }

   if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
   } else {
      init();
   }

})();
